- **Documents sheet**: 2203 transitions analyzed
- **Library sheet**: 2004 transitions analyzed

|------|-------|
| Sheet Structural | 25 |
| Column Structural | 415 |
| Row Structural | 1188 |
| Cell Edit | 1550 |
| Style Only | 71 |
| Identical | 748 |
| **Non-identical transitions** | **2097** |
